codex
FACTORIAL_RATIONALE

v160 changed two factors together for E16:

1. FC1 BMM order: `gate -> up` became `up -> gate`
2. SwiGLU survivor buffer: `gate_output` became `up_output`

v165 extended both factors to E32/E64 and regressed every shape, so the mechanism is confounded. A single E32 isolation is novel enough because it tests the storage/lifetime hypothesis independently from BMM launch order, without re-running the full factorial grid.

SELECTED_EXPERIMENT

Base: `v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py`

Create v166 with only this behavioral change:

- E16: keep v160 path exactly: `up -> gate`, SwiGLU writes into `up_output`, delete `gate_output`
- E32: restore original BMM order: `gate -> up`, but write SwiGLU into `up_output`, delete `gate_output`
- E64: keep original v160 non-E16 path exactly: `gate -> up`, SwiGLU writes into `gate_output`, delete `up_output`

No E64 test and no alternate E32 order/storage permutation unless this E32 storage-only experiment is positive.

MODIFICATION_POINTS

In [v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py](/D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py:384), split the existing `else` into explicit E32 and E64 behavior:

```python
if num_experts == 16:
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, up_output)
    del gate_output
    activation = up_output
elif num_experts == 32:
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, up_output)
    del gate_output
    activation = up_output
else:
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, gate_output)
    del up_output
    activation = gate_output
```

MATH_ALIAS_PROOF

SwiGLU computes elementwise:

`activation = silu(gate_output) * up_output`

For each element, output may alias either input if the kernel reads both input values before storing the result. Existing v160 already validates output aliasing into `up_output` for E16, and the original path validates aliasing into `gate_output`.

For E32 v166:

- `gate_output` remains live as read-only input to `swiglu`
- `up_output` is both read input and write output
- after `swiglu`, only `activation = up_output` is consumed by FC2
- `gate_output` is deleted only after the SwiGLU call

So the mathematical value passed to FC2 is unchanged from baseline.

SHAPE_ISOLATION

E32 is the right single isolation because:

- v165 showed E32 regression under both changes: about `+0.041 ms`, `+1.12%`
- E16 already has the positive/confounded v160 signal
- E64 is larger and already regressed under v165, making it higher risk and less diagnostic
- E32 uses `expert_chunk = 32`, so allocator/lifetime behavior can differ materially from E16/E64

EXPECTED_SIGNAL

Do not expect a 3% gain. Expected value is diagnostic:

- positive E32 result: storage target/lifetime may be shape-specific useful, while up-first BMM order caused v165 regression
- neutral result: E16 win was likely noise or E16-specific allocator/order interaction
- negative result: `up_output` survivor is itself harmful for E32, so abandon this factor outside E16

RISKS

- Allocator timing may be noisy because both FC1 tensors are same shape and lifetime differences are small.
- In-place alias correctness depends on `swiglu` safely supporting output aliasing with `up_output`; v160 E16 suggests yes, but correctness must still be rechecked.
- A single E32 result may not generalize, but that is acceptable here because the goal is resolving the v160/v165 confound, not broad promotion.

IMPLEMENTATION

Ready patch target: copy `v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py` to `v166_e32_storage_only.py`, then apply the branch split above.

I could not write the file in this session because the workspace is read-only.

codex
E16_IDENTITY: PASS  
`num_experts == 16` branch is byte-identical to v160 behavior: up BMM, gate BMM, SwiGLU writes `up_output`, delete `gate_output`.

E32_FACTOR: PASS  
E32 is the approved storage-only factor: gate BMM first, up BMM second, SwiGLU writes `up_output`, delete `gate_output`, `activation = up_output`.

E64_IDENTITY: PASS  
`else` branch remains v160 E64 behavior: gate BMM, up BMM, SwiGLU writes `gate_output`, delete `up_output`.

MATH_ALIAS: PASS  
SwiGLU reads `gate_output` and `up_output` elementwise, then stores the same element to `activation`. Aliasing `activation` with `up_output` is already used by v160 E16 and is safe for this E32 branch because `gate_output` stays live through the call and is deleted only afterward.

PADDING: PASS  
No pack/unpack/group offset logic changed. SwiGLU still guards rows with `if i < group_size`; unpack still writes only valid rows. Padding behavior is inherited from v160.

NO_UNLISTED_DIFF: PASS  
`git diff --no-index v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py v166_e32_storage_only.py` shows exactly one hunk: insertion of the E32 branch. No other code changes.

BLOCKERS: NONE
